^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  232)     """

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  235) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  236)     locs = {} if locals is None else locals
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  237) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  238)     # In order of debuggers like PDB being able to step through the code,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  239)     # we add a fake linecache entry.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  240)     count = 1
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  241)     base_filename = filename
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  242)     while True:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  243)         linecache_tuple = (
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  244)             len(script),
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  245)             None,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  246)             script.splitlines(True),
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  247)             filename,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  248)         )

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  250)         if old_val == linecache_tuple:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  251)             break

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  253)         filename = f"{base_filename[:-1]}-{count}>"
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  254)         count += 1
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  255) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  256)     _compile_and_eval(script, globs, locs, filename)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  257) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  258)     return locs

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  261) def _make_attr_tuple_class(cls_name: str, attr_names: list[str]) -> type:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  262)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  263)     Create a tuple subclass to hold `Attribute`s for an `attrs` class.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  264) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  265)     The subclass is a bare tuple with properties for names.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  266) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  267)     class MyClassAttributes(tuple):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  268)         __slots__ = ()
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  269)         x = property(itemgetter(0))
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  270)     """

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  272)     body = {}
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  273)     for i, attr_name in enumerate(attr_names):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  274) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  275)         def getter(self, i=i):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  276)             return self[i]
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  277) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  278)         body[attr_name] = property(getter)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  279)     return type(attr_class_name, (tuple,), body)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  280) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  281) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  282) # Tuple class for extracted attributes from a class definition.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  283) # `base_attrs` is a subset of `attrs`.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  284) class _Attributes(NamedTuple):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  285)     attrs: type

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  289) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  290) def _is_class_var(annot):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  291)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  292)     Check whether *annot* is a typing.ClassVar.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  293) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  294)     The string comparison hack is used to avoid evaluating all string
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  295)     annotations which would put attrs-based classes at a performance
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  296)     disadvantage compared to plain old classes.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  297)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  298)     annot = str(annot)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  299) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  300)     # Annotation can be quoted.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  301)     if annot.startswith(("'", '"')) and annot.endswith(("'", '"')):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  302)         annot = annot[1:-1]
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  303) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  304)     return annot.startswith(_CLASSVAR_PREFIXES)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  305) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  306) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  307) def _has_own_attribute(cls, attrib_name):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  308)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  309)     Check whether *cls* defines *attrib_name* (and doesn't just inherit it).
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  310)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  311)     return attrib_name in cls.__dict__
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  312) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  313) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  314) def _collect_base_attrs(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  315)     cls, taken_attr_names
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  316) ) -> tuple[list[Attribute], dict[str, type]]:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  317)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  318)     Collect attr.ibs from base classes of *cls*, except *taken_attr_names*.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  319)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  320)     base_attrs = []
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  321)     base_attr_map = {}  # A dictionary of base attrs to their classes.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  322) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  323)     # Traverse the MRO and collect attributes.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  324)     for base_cls in reversed(cls.__mro__[1:-1]):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  325)         for a in getattr(base_cls, "__attrs_attrs__", []):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  326)             if a.inherited or a.name in taken_attr_names:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  327)                 continue
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  328) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  329)             a = a.evolve(inherited=True)  # noqa: PLW2901
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  330)             base_attrs.append(a)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  331)             base_attr_map[a.name] = base_cls
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  332) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  333)     # For each name, only keep the freshest definition i.e. the furthest at the
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  334)     # back.  base_attr_map is fine because it gets overwritten with every new
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  335)     # instance.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  336)     filtered = []
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  337)     seen = set()
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  338)     for a in reversed(base_attrs):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  339)         if a.name in seen:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  340)             continue
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  341)         filtered.insert(0, a)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  342)         seen.add(a.name)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  343) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  344)     return filtered, base_attr_map
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  345) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  346) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  347) def _collect_base_attrs_broken(cls, taken_attr_names):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  348)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  349)     Collect attr.ibs from base classes of *cls*, except *taken_attr_names*.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  350) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  351)     N.B. *taken_attr_names* will be mutated.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  352) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  353)     Adhere to the old incorrect behavior.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  354) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  355)     Notably it collects from the front and considers inherited attributes which
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  356)     leads to the buggy behavior reported in #428.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  357)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  358)     base_attrs = []
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  359)     base_attr_map = {}  # A dictionary of base attrs to their classes.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  360) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  361)     # Traverse the MRO and collect attributes.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  362)     for base_cls in cls.__mro__[1:-1]:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  363)         for a in getattr(base_cls, "__attrs_attrs__", []):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  364)             if a.name in taken_attr_names:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  365)                 continue
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  366) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  367)             a = a.evolve(inherited=True)  # noqa: PLW2901
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  368)             taken_attr_names.add(a.name)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  369)             base_attrs.append(a)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  370)             base_attr_map[a.name] = base_cls
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  371) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  372)     return base_attrs, base_attr_map
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  373) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  374) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  375) def _transform_attrs(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  376)     cls, these, auto_attribs, kw_only, collect_by_mro, field_transformer
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  377) ) -> _Attributes:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  378)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  379)     Transform all `_CountingAttr`s on a class into `Attribute`s.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  380) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  381)     If *these* is passed, use that and don't look for them on the class.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  382) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  383)     If *collect_by_mro* is True, collect them in the correct MRO order,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  384)     otherwise use the old -- incorrect -- order.  See #428.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  385) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  386)     Return an `_Attributes`.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  387)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  388)     cd = cls.__dict__
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  389)     anns = _get_annotations(cls)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  390) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  391)     if these is not None:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  392)         ca_list = list(these.items())
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  393)     elif auto_attribs is True:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  394)         ca_names = {
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  395)             name
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  396)             for name, attr in cd.items()
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  397)             if attr.__class__ is _CountingAttr
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  398)         }
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  399)         ca_list = []
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  400)         annot_names = set()
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  401)         for attr_name, type in anns.items():
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  402)             if _is_class_var(type):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  403)                 continue
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  404)             annot_names.add(attr_name)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  405)             a = cd.get(attr_name, NOTHING)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  406) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  407)             if a.__class__ is not _CountingAttr:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  408)                 a = attrib(a)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  409)             ca_list.append((attr_name, a))
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  410) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  411)         unannotated = ca_names - annot_names
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  412)         if unannotated:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  413)             raise UnannotatedAttributeError(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  414)                 "The following `attr.ib`s lack a type annotation: "
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  415)                 + ", ".join(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  416)                     sorted(unannotated, key=lambda n: cd.get(n).counter)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  417)                 )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  418)                 + "."
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  419)             )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  420)     else:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  421)         ca_list = sorted(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  422)             (
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  423)                 (name, attr)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  424)                 for name, attr in cd.items()
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  425)                 if attr.__class__ is _CountingAttr
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  426)             ),
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  427)             key=lambda e: e[1].counter,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  428)         )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  429) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  430)     fca = Attribute.from_counting_attr
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  431)     own_attrs = [
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  432)         fca(attr_name, ca, anns.get(attr_name)) for attr_name, ca in ca_list
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  433)     ]
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  434) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  435)     if collect_by_mro:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  436)         base_attrs, base_attr_map = _collect_base_attrs(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  437)             cls, {a.name for a in own_attrs}
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  438)         )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  439)     else:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  440)         base_attrs, base_attr_map = _collect_base_attrs_broken(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  441)             cls, {a.name for a in own_attrs}
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  442)         )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  443) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  444)     if kw_only:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  445)         own_attrs = [a.evolve(kw_only=True) for a in own_attrs]
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  446)         base_attrs = [a.evolve(kw_only=True) for a in base_attrs]
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  447) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  448)     attrs = base_attrs + own_attrs
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  449) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  450)     if field_transformer is not None:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  451)         attrs = tuple(field_transformer(cls, attrs))
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  452) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  453)     # Check attr order after executing the field_transformer.

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  563) def evolve(*args, **changes):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  564)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  565)     Create a new instance, based on the first positional argument with
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  566)     *changes* applied.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  567) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  568)     .. tip::
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  569) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  570)        On Python 3.13 and later, you can also use `copy.replace` instead.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  571) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  572)     Args:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  573)

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  580) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  581)     Returns:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  582)         A copy of inst with *changes* incorporated.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  583) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  584)     Raises:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  587) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  588)         attrs.exceptions.NotAnAttrsClassError:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  591)     .. versionadded:: 17.1.0
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  592)     .. deprecated:: 23.1.0
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  593)        It is now deprecated to pass the instance using the keyword argument
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  594)        *inst*. It will raise a warning until at least April 2024, after which
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  595)        it will become an error. Always pass the instance as a positional
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  596)        argument.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  597)     .. versionchanged:: 24.1.0
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  598)        *inst* can't be passed as a keyword argument anymore.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  599)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  600)     try:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  601)         (inst,) = args
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  602)     except ValueError:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  603)         msg = (
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  604)             f"evolve() takes 1 positional argument, but {len(args)} were given"
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  605)         )
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  606)         raise TypeError(msg) from None
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  607) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  608)     cls = inst.__class__
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  609)     attrs = fields(cls)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  610)     for a in attrs:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  612)             continue

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  943) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  944)         # The following is a fix for
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  945)         # <https://github.com/python-attrs/attrs/issues/102>.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  946)         # If a method mentions `__class__` or uses the no-arg super(), the
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  947)         # compiler will bake a reference to the class in the method itself

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  950)         for item in itertools.chain(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  951)             cls.__dict__.values(), additional_closure_functions_to_update
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  952)         ):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  953)             if isinstance(item, (classmethod, staticmethod)):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  954)                 # Class- and staticmethods hide their functions inside.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  955)                 # These might need to be rewritten as well.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  956)                 closure_cells = getattr(item.__func__, "__closure__", None)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  957)             elif isinstance(item, property):
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  958)                 # Workaround for property `super()` shortcut (PY3-only).
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  959)                 # There is no universal way for other descriptors.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  960)                 closure_cells = getattr(item.fget, "__closure__", None)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  961)             else:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  962)                 closure_cells = getattr(item, "__closure__", None)
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  963) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  964)             if not closure_cells:  # Catch None or the empty list.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  965)                 continue
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  966)             for cell in closure_cells:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  967)                 try:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  971)                     pass
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200  972)                 else:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1225)     if order is None:

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1340)             automatically detect that.  This argument allows to set a custom
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1341)             name for a more meaningful ``repr`` output.  This argument is
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1342)             pointless in Python 3 and is therefore deprecated.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1343) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1344)     .. caution::
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1345)         Refer to `attrs.define` for the rest of the parameters, but note that they
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1346)         can have different defaults.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1347) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1348)         Notably, leaving *on_setattr* as `None` will **not** add any hooks.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1349)

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1765)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1766)     Create the source and globs for a __repr__ and return it.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1767)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1768)     # Figure out which attributes to include, and which function to use to
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1769)     # format them. The a.repr value can be either bool or a custom
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1770)     # callable.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1771)     attr_names_with_reprs = tuple(
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1772)         (a.name, (repr if a.repr is True else a.repr), a.init)

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1778)     }
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1779)     globs["_compat"] = _compat
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 1780)     globs["AttributeError"] = AttributeError

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2365) class Attribute:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2366)     """
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2367)     *Read-only* representation of an attribute.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2368) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2369)     .. warning::
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2370) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2371)        You should never instantiate this class yourself.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2372) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2373)     The class has *all* arguments of `attr.ib` (except for ``factory`` which is
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2374)     only syntactic sugar for ``default=Factory(...)`` plus the following:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2375) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2376)     - ``name`` (`str`): The name of the attribute.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2377)     - ``alias`` (`str`): The __init__ parameter name of the attribute, after
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2378)       any explicit overrides and default private-attribute-name handling.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2379)     - ``inherited`` (`bool`): Whether or not that attribute has been inherited
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2380)       from a base class.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2381)     - ``eq_key`` and ``order_key`` (`typing.Callable` or `None`): The
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2382)       callables that are used for comparing and ordering objects by this
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2383)       attribute, respectively. These are set by passing a callable to
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2384)       `attr.ib`'s ``eq``, ``order``, or ``cmp`` arguments. See also
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2385)       :ref:`comparison customization <custom-comparison>`.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2386) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2387)     Instances of this class are frequently used for introspection purposes
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2388)     like:
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2389) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2390)     - `fields` returns a tuple of them.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2391)     - Validators get them passed as the first argument.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2392)     - The :ref:`field transformer <transform-fields>` hook receives a list of
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2393)       them.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2394)     - The ``alias`` property exposes the __init__ parameter name of the field,
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2395)       with any overrides and default private-attribute handling applied.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2396) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2397) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2398)     .. versionadded:: 20.1.0 *inherited*

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2400)     .. versionchanged:: 20.2.0 *inherited* is not taken into account for

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2404) 
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2405)     For the full version history of the fields, see `attr.ib`.
^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2406)     """

^6a8b836 (evals 2026-10-22 10:53:58 +0200 2941)         are valid identifiers. ``make_class()`` will *not* validate them for
